\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot r=0.

\mathcal{X}=0
\mathcal{X}=1
\mathcal{X}=x_{+}
(r,\bar{\mathfrak{v}})
(r_{1},\mathbf{v}_{1})
(r_{k},\mathbf{v}_{k})

(b)

\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot r\neq 0,\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0.

Figure 4.
The backward progress in a slab.

Lemma 5.4
.

For
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}\leq 0
,
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot r\neq 0
and
t_{1,0}\leq s\leq t_{1}
, one has

(5.21)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\left|\frac{\partial(\mathcal{X},\Phi)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}}(s)\right|=

\displaystyle\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}(s-t_{1,0})(t-s)\cdot\left|\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\right|.

Proof.

We divide the proof into several steps.

Step 1. Calculation on
\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}(s)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{i}}
.
It follows from (
5.16
)–(
5.17
) that

(5.22)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{ds}[\mathcal{X}(s)\mathcal{V}_{1}(s)]=\mathcal{V}_{1}^{2}(s)+\mathcal{V}_{2}^{2}(s)=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2},

which yields that

(5.23)

\displaystyle r\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}+|\mathcal{V}_{1}(t_{1})|=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}(t-t_{1})\quad\text{and}\quad|\mathcal{V}_{1}(t_{1})|=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}(t_{1}-t_{1,0}).

For
s\in(t_{1,0},t_{1}]
, it holds that
\mathcal{V}_{1}(s)\geq 0
. Then one has from (
5.22
) that

(5.24)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}(s)\mathcal{V}_{1}(s)=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}(s-t_{1,0}),

which, together with (
5.18
), yields that

(5.25)

\displaystyle\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\mathcal{X}^{2}(s)-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}(s-t_{1,0}).

Thus we have from (
5.25
) that

(5.26)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}^{2}(s)=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}(s-t_{1,0})^{2}+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r^{2}.

Applying
\partial_{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}
to (
5.26
), one has

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{X}(s)\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}(s)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}=2\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}(s-t_{1,0})-2\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2},

which yields that

(5.27)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}(s)\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}(s)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}=-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}(s-t_{1,0})+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2}.

Applying
\partial_{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}
to (
5.26
), one gets

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{X}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}

\displaystyle=2\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}(s-t_{1,0})+2\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r^{2}-2\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{3}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2}

\displaystyle=2\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}(s-t_{1,0})+2\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2},

which yields that

(5.28)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}=\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}(s-t_{1,0})+\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2}.

Step 2. Calculation on
\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}.
It follows from (
5.19
) that

\displaystyle\Phi(s)

\displaystyle=\phi-\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}(\tau)\,d\tau=\phi-\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}(\tau)\,d\tau-\int_{s}^{t_{1}}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle=\phi-\int_{1}^{r}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{-1}{|\mathcal{V}_{1}|}\,d\mathcal{X}-\int_{\mathcal{X}(s)}^{1}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{|\mathcal{V}_{1}|}\,d\mathcal{X}

(5.29)

\displaystyle=\phi-\int_{r}^{1}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{|\mathcal{V}_{1}|}\,d\mathcal{X}-\int_{\mathcal{X}(s)}^{1}\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{|\mathcal{V}_{1}|}\,d\mathcal{X}.

To calculate (
5.29
), we consider the change of variable
\displaystyle y=y(z)=\frac{z}{r}\sqrt{1+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}}=z\cdot\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|r},
and define
(y(r),y(1),y(\mathcal{X}(s)))=\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|r}(r,1,\mathcal{X}(s))
. Then one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{V}_{2}(z)}{z}\cdot\frac{1}{|\mathcal{V}_{1}(z)|}dz=\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot r}{z^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}\cdot r^{2}}{z^{2}}}}dz

\displaystyle=\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot r}{y^{2}r^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}\cdot|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{y^{2}}}}\cdot\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|r}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|}dy

(5.30)

\displaystyle=\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{1}{y\sqrt{y^{2}-1}}\,dy.

Applying (
5.30
) to (
5.29
), one obtains that

(5.31)

\displaystyle\Phi(s)=\phi-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\int_{y(r)}^{y(1)}\frac{1}{y\sqrt{y^{2}-1}}\,dy-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\int_{y(\mathcal{X}(s))}^{y(1)}\frac{1}{y\sqrt{y^{2}-1}}\,dy.

Thus we have from (
5.31
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Phi(s)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}=-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}

\displaystyle\left\{2\frac{\partial y(1)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(1)\sqrt{y(1)^{2}-1}}-\frac{\partial y(r)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(r)\sqrt{y(r)^{2}-1}}\right.

(5.32)

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\frac{\partial y(\mathcal{X}(s))}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(\mathcal{X}(s))\sqrt{y(\mathcal{X}(s))^{2}-1}}\right\}.

A direct calculation shows

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\partial y(1)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(1)\sqrt{y(1)^{2}-1}}=\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}=-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}},\\
&\frac{\partial y(r)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(r)\sqrt{y(r)^{2}-1}}=\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{r^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}=-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|},\\
&\frac{\partial y(\mathcal{X}(s))}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(\mathcal{X}(s))\sqrt{y(\mathcal{X}(s))^{2}-1}}\\
\ &\qquad\qquad=\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}+\frac{1}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}},\end{split}

which, together with (
5.32
), yields that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}=\,

\displaystyle\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\left\{\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\left[\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\right]\right.

(5.33)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\frac{1}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\right\}

Step 3. Calculation on
\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}
.
We have from (
5.31
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}=-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}

\displaystyle\left\{2\frac{\partial y(1)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(1)\sqrt{y(1)^{2}-1}}-\frac{\partial y(r)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(r)\sqrt{y(r)^{2}-1}}\right.

(5.34)

\displaystyle\left.\quad-\frac{\partial y(\mathcal{X}(s))}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(\mathcal{X}(s))\sqrt{y(\mathcal{X}(s))^{2}-1}}\right\}.

A direct calculation shows

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\partial y(1)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(1)\sqrt{y(1)^{2}-1}}=-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}},\\
&\frac{\partial y(r)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(r)\sqrt{y(r)^{2}-1}}=-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}=-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\cdot\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|},\\
&\frac{\partial y(\mathcal{X}(s))}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{y(\mathcal{X}(s))\sqrt{y(\mathcal{X}(s))^{2}-1}}\\
&\qquad\qquad=-\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}+\frac{1}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}},\end{split}

which, together with (
5.34
), yields that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}=\,

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\left[\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\right]

(5.35)

\displaystyle+\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{1}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}.

Step 4. Conclusion.
For simplicity of presentation, we denote

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}.

It follows from (
5.27
), (
5.28
), (
5.33
) and (
5.35
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\left[\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}-\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{X}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\cdot\left\{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\mathbf{E}+\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{1}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathcal{X}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\cdot\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\left\{\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\mathbf{E}+\frac{1}{\mathcal{X}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\left\{-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}(s-t_{1,0})+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2}\right\}\cdot\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle\quad-\left\{\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|(s-t_{1,0})^{2}-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}(s-t_{1,0})+\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r^{2}\right\}\cdot\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\mathbf{E}

(5.36)

\displaystyle=-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}(s-t_{1,0})\left\{(s-t_{1,0})-\left[\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\right]\right\}\cdot\mathbf{E}.

Using (
5.23
), one has that

(5.37)

\displaystyle t_{1,0}=t-\left(\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r+\frac{2}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}\right).

A direct calculation shows

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}&=-\frac{1}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r+2\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r-\frac{4|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}+\frac{2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}},\\
\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}&=\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot\left[-\frac{2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}||\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}r+\frac{4|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{4}}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}-\frac{2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|(1-r^{2})}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}}\right],\end{split}

which yields that

\displaystyle\text{sgn}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}-|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|\frac{\partial t_{1,0}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r-\frac{2|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r+\frac{4}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}-\frac{2(|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2})}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}}

(5.38)

\displaystyle=-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}r+\frac{2}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}\sqrt{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}r^{2}}=t-t_{1,0}.

Substituting (
5.38
) into (
5.36
), one obtains

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\left[\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}-\frac{\partial\mathcal{X}}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}}\frac{\partial\Phi}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}}\right]=-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}(s-t_{1,0})\left[(s-t_{1,0})-(t-t_{1,0})\right]\cdot\mathbf{E},

which concludes (
5.21
) immediately. Therefore the proof of Lemma
5.4
is completed.
∎

Assume for
t\in[0,\tau^{\delta}]
,

(5.39)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\varepsilon^{4}h^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq 1,\quad\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\varepsilon f_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\leq 1.

We have the following estimate.

Lemma 5.5
.

Let
t\in[0,\tau^{\delta}]
,
\mathbf{k}\geq 6,
it holds that

(5.40)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\varepsilon^{3}h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(t)\{\|\varepsilon^{3}h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{N-1}+\varepsilon^{\mathfrak{b}}\}+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

Since the proof for Lemma
5.5
is quite long. We divide it into several steps.

Step 1.
Integrating along the backward characteristic, we have from (
5.15
) that

\displaystyle h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(t,r,\phi,v)=

\displaystyle h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0,\mathcal{X}(0),\Phi(0),\mathcal{V}(0))\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}t\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{K}_{M,\tilde{w}}h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s,\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),\mathcal{V}(s))\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}\,ds

(5.41)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}\,ds.

Noting (
4.7
), by a simple iterative argument, one gets

\displaystyle\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{K}_{M,\tilde{w}}h^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{K}_{M}\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{k}-1}}+\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq...

(5.42)

\displaystyle\leq\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}},

where
\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}:=\frac{1}{\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}}h^{\varepsilon}_{R}=\frac{F_{R}^{\varepsilon}}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}
. The norms
\|g\|_{L^{\infty}}
,
\|g\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}_{\beta}}
and
\|g\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}
mean respectively
\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{r,\phi,\bar{\mathfrak{v}}}}
(or equivalently
\|g\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{1}\times\mathbb{R}^{2})}
),
\|\tilde{w}_{\beta}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{r,\phi,\bar{\mathfrak{v}}}}
and
\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{r,\phi}L^{2}_{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}}}
.

Step 2.
To close (
5.42
), we still need to estimate
\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(\bar{\mathfrak{v}})|^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}.
Similar to (
5.41
), one has

\displaystyle\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(t,r,\phi,\bar{\mathfrak{v}})=

\displaystyle\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0,\mathcal{X}(0),\Phi(0),\mathcal{V}(0))\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}t\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{K}_{M}\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s,\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),\mathcal{V}(s))\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}\,ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\bar{S}_{R}\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}\,ds.

It is obvious that

(5.43)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0,\mathcal{X}(0),\Phi(0),\mathcal{V}(0))|^{2}\exp\{-\frac{2}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}t\}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}\leq C\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2},\\
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}\bar{S}_{R}\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}\,ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}\leq\varepsilon^{4}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.\end{split}

Next we consider

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{K}_{M}\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s,\mathcal{X}_{cl}(s),\Phi_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}.

Since we need
\|\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}
, we only consider
\mathcal{P}
for
r\neq 0
. We divide the discussion of
\mathcal{P}
into several cases.

Case 1.
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\geq\mathfrak{N}
.
Denote
\Theta_{1}:=\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\geq\mathfrak{N}\}}
. Noting
|\mathcal{V}_{cl}(s)|=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\geq\mathfrak{N}\}}\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\Theta_{1}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}(1+|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|)^{-\mathbf{k}-1}\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

(5.44)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mathfrak{N}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Case 2.
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\geq 2\mathfrak{N}
.
Denote
\Theta_{2}:=\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\geq 2\mathfrak{N}\}}.
Since
|\mathcal{V}_{cl}(s)|=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|
, then it holds that
|\mathcal{V}_{cl}(s)-v^{\prime}|\geq\mathfrak{N}
. In this case, we have from (
5.9
) that there exists a positive constant
c_{4}>0
such that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\geq 2\mathfrak{N}\}}\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-c_{4}\mathfrak{N}^{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{2}k_{M}(\mathcal{V}_{cl}(s),v^{\prime})e^{c_{4}|\mathcal{V}_{cl}-v^{\prime}|^{2}}(1+v^{\prime}|^{-\mathbf{k}})\cdot\exp\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)\}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

(5.45)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mathfrak{N}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Case 3.
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\leq 2\mathfrak{N}
and further assume:
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}/\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\leq\bar{m},
or
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\leq\bar{m}
where
\bar{m}\ll 1
.
Denote the corresponding indicative function as
\Theta_{3}
. Thus
we have

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|\leq\bar{m}\mathfrak{N}\quad\text{or}\quad|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\leq\bar{m},

which yields that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{3}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\Theta_{3}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

(5.46)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(1+|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|)^{-\mathbf{k}-1}\Theta_{3}(\bar{\mathfrak{v}})\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}\leq C\bar{m}\mathfrak{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Case 4.
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\leq 2\mathfrak{N},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}/\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m},
and
|s-t_{1,0}|\leq m_{1}
or
t-s\leq m_{1}
.
Denote the corresponding indicative function as
\Theta_{4}
. The length of time integral domain is less than
2m_{1}
, hence

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{4}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\Theta_{4}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

(5.47)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}\Theta_{4}\,ds\right\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}\leq C\frac{m_{1}^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Case 5.
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\leq 2\mathfrak{N},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}/\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m}
and
|s-t_{1,0}|\geq m_{1},t-s\geq m_{1}
. We also assume

\mathcal{X}(s)\in[(1-m_{2})r,(1+m_{2})r]\quad\text{with}\quad m_{2}\ll\bar{m}\ll 1.

Denote the corresponding indicative function as
\Theta_{5}
.

Noting
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}/\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m}
, one has

\displaystyle 1-x_{+}=1-\frac{r}{\sqrt{1+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}}}\geq 1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}}}\geq\frac{\bar{m}^{2}}{6}.

Since for
l\geq 1
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{t_{l+1}}^{t_{l}}|\mathcal{V}_{1}|\,ds=2(1-x_{+}),

which yields that

\displaystyle t_{l}-t_{l+1}\geq\frac{\bar{m}^{2}}{3\mathfrak{N}},

and the number of collisions
k\leq k_{max}=(3\mathfrak{N}t)/\bar{m}^{2}.

We also have that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{V}_{1}(s)|^{2}=|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}\frac{r^{2}}{\mathcal{X}^{2}(s)}\in\left[|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{(1-m_{2})^{2}},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}-\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{(1+m_{2})^{2}}\right],

which, together with
0<m_{2}\ll\bar{m}\ll 1
, yields that

(5.48)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{V}_{1}(s)|^{2}\geq\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}-\frac{3m_{2}}{(1-m_{2})^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}\geq\left[1-\frac{3m_{2}}{\bar{m}^{2}(1-m_{2})^{2}}\right]\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}\geq\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}{4}.

Thus we have from (
5.48
) and (
5.16
) that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d\mathcal{X}}{ds}\right|=|\mathcal{V}_{1}|\geq\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{2}\geq\frac{\bar{m}^{2}}{2}.

Then we use change of variable

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{5}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\Theta_{5}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}(t-s)}\Theta_{5}ds\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq Ck\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{0}^{1}\Theta_{5}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{V}_{1}|}d\mathcal{X}\right\}^{2}

(5.49)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\mathfrak{N}t}{\bar{m}^{6}}\left(\frac{m_{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\right)^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Case 6.
|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|\leq\mathfrak{N},|v^{\prime}|\leq 2\mathfrak{N},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}/\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m},|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|\geq\bar{m}
,
|s-t_{1,0}|\geq m_{1}
and
t-s\geq m_{1}
. We also assume

(5.50)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\leq(1-m_{2})r\quad\text{or}\quad\mathcal{X}\geq(1+m_{2})r.

Denote the corresponding indicative function as
\Theta_{6}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{6}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\Theta_{6}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}},

and divide
\mathcal{P}_{6}=\mathcal{P}_{61}+\mathcal{P}_{62}
for
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0
and
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0
. We rewrite
\mathcal{P}_{61},\mathcal{P}_{62}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{61}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{\left(\int_{t_{1}}^{t}+\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}ds\right\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{62}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{\left(\int_{t_{1,0}}^{t}+\int_{0}^{t_{1,0}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}ds\right\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}.

For
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0
, one gets

(5.51)

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu}(t-t_{1})\geq 1-x_{+}\geq\frac{\bar{m}^{2}}{6}.

It follows from (
5.51
) that

(5.52)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{1}}e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}ds\leq\int_{0}^{t_{1}}e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t_{1}-s)}ds\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\cdot\frac{\bar{m}^{2}}{6}}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}e^{-\frac{1}{6\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}},

which yields that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{61}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+Ce^{-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

(5.53)

\displaystyle=:\mathcal{P}_{61R}+Ce^{-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

For
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0
, one has

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu}(t-t_{1,0})\geq 1-x_{+}\geq\frac{\bar{m}^{2}}{6},

then similar to (
5.52
)–(
5.53
), one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{62}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{t_{1,0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+Ce^{-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

(5.54)

\displaystyle=:\mathcal{P}_{62R}+Ce^{-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Estimate for
\mathcal{P}_{61R}
. We rewrite
\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})
as
\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathbf{X}(s),v^{\prime})
, with

(5.55)

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{1}(s)=\mathcal{X}(s)\cos\Phi(s),\quad\mathbf{X}_{2}(s)=\mathcal{X}(s)\sin\Phi(s).

Noting for
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0
and
s\geq t_{1}
, there is no collision with the boundary. Then it is better to use the original coordinate. From (
5.8
), we actually have

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{1}(s)=r\cos\phi-v_{1}(t-s),\quad\mathbf{X}_{2}(s)=r\sin\phi-v_{2}(t-s),

which yields that

(5.56)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial{\mathbf{X}}}{{\partial v}}\right|=(t-s)^{2}\geq m_{1}^{2}.

Hence one obtains

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{61R}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}(t-s)}ds\right\}^{2}d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\tilde{k}_{M}(\mathcal{V}(s),v^{\prime})\mathbf{f}_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathcal{X}(s),\Phi(s),v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}\right\}^{2}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}_{0}(t-s)}dsd\bar{\mathfrak{v}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathfrak{N}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathbf{X}(s),v^{\prime})|^{2}dv^{\prime}\right\}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}_{0}(t-s)}dsdv

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{m_{1}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}>0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s,\mathbf{X},v^{\prime})|^{2}dv^{\prime}\right\}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\tilde{\nu}_{0}(t-s)}\,d\mathbf{X}\,ds

(5.57)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{m_{1}^{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Estimate for
\mathcal{P}_{62R}
. For
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0
and
s\in(t_{1},t],
since there is no collision with
\partial B_{1}
, then by similar arguments in (
5.56
)–(
5.57
), we have

(5.58)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\Theta_{6}\mathbf{1}_{\{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0\}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\left\{\int_{t_{1}}^{t}(\cdot\cdot\cdot)\right\}^{2}\,d\bar{\mathfrak{v}}\leq\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{m_{1}^{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}<0,s\in(t_{1,0},t_{1}]
and
\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}\cdot r\neq 0
, we use Lemma
5.4
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\left|\frac{\partial(\mathcal{X},\Phi)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}}(s)\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}(s-t_{1,0})(t-s)\times\left(\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}-1}}\right)

(5.59)

\displaystyle=(s-t_{1,0})(t-s)\times\left(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}\right).

For the case of
\mathcal{X}^{2}/r^{2}\geq(1+m_{2})^{2}
, it holds

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\left(1+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}\right)-\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}\geq(1+m_{2})^{2},

which yields that

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}

(5.60)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}\geq 1-\frac{1}{1+m_{2}}\geq\frac{m_{2}}{2}.

For the case of
\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\leq(1-m_{2})^{2},
one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}\left(1+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}\right)-\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}\leq(1-m_{2})^{2},\quad\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(1+\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}\right)-\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}^{2}}{\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}^{2}}\geq 1,

which yields that

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{X}^{2}}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{r^{2}}(1+\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}})-\frac{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{2}|^{2}}{|\bar{\mathfrak{v}}_{1}|^{2}}}}

(5.61)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1-m_{2}}-1\geq m_{2}.

Hence it follows from (
5.59
)–(
5.61
) that

(5.62)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}\left|\frac{\partial(\mathcal{X},\Phi)}{\partial\bar{\mathfrak{v}}}(s)\right|\geq\frac{m_{2}}{2}(s-t_{1,0})(t-s)\geq\frac{m_{2}}{2}m_{1}^{2}.

Using (
5.56
) and (
5.62
), similar to (
5.57
), one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{62R}\leq\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{m_{1}^{2}m_{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which, together with (
5.53
), (
5.54
) and (
5.57
), yields that

(5.63)

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{6}\leq\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{m_{1}^{2}m_{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+Ce^{-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

We have from (
5.44
)–(
5.47
), (
5.49
) and (
5.63
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{N}}+C\left(\bar{m}\mathfrak{N}+Ce^{-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon^{2}}\bar{m}^{2}}\right)\right\}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(Cm_{1}^{2}+\frac{C\mathfrak{N}t}{\bar{m}^{6}}m_{2}^{2}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{4}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{m_{1}^{2}m_{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Choosing
\bar{m}=\frac{1}{\mathfrak{N}^{2}}
and
\mathfrak{N}>1
suitably large, and
m_{1}=\bar{m}\varepsilon^{2},m_{2}=\bar{m}^{4}\varepsilon^{2}
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}\leq o(1)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{\varepsilon^{6}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which, together with (
5.43
), yields that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{f}^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+o(1)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\mathfrak{N}}}{\varepsilon^{6}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(5.64)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{4}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Substituting (
5.64
) into (
5.42
), one has

(5.65)

\displaystyle\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Noting the definition of
\bar{S}_{R}
in (
5.10
), one has

\displaystyle|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R,1}|

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R,2}|

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{-1}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}},

\displaystyle|\tilde{\nu}^{-1}\tilde{w}_{\mathbf{k}}\bar{S}_{R,3}|

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon^{N-6}+\varepsilon^{\mathfrak{b}-5}),

which together with (
5.65
) and (
5.39
) yields (
5.40
). Therefore the proof of Lemma
5.5
is finished.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.5
:
Applying (
5.40
) to (
5.11
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{B_{1}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{\tilde{\nu}}^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq C(\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1)\times\left\{1+\varepsilon^{\min\{2\mathbf{k}-5,8\}}\left[\|h^{\varepsilon}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{6}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right]\right\}.

Since
\mathbf{k}\geq 6
, we use Gronwall inequality and get

(5.66)

\displaystyle\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\exp\left\{\left(1+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f^{\varepsilon}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)t\right\}\leq C(t).

It follows from (
5.66
) and (
5.40
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(\varepsilon^{3}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}})\leq C(t)\{1+\varepsilon^{3}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}\}\leq C(t).

Taking
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
for some small
\varepsilon_{0}\ll 1
, we close the
a priori
assumption (
5.39
) directly. Therefore the proof of Theorem
1.5
is completed.
\hfill\Box

6.
Appendix

6.1.
Proof of Lemma
2.6

We divide the proof into several steps.

Step 1.
For later use, we define the corresponding Burnett functions:

(6.1)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{A}_{11}^{0}=\frac{1}{2T^{0}}\left\{(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})^{2}-(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})^{2}\right\}\sqrt{\mu_{0}},\quad\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0}=\frac{1}{T^{0}}\left\{(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})\right\}\sqrt{\mu_{0}},\\
\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0}=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2T^{0}}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\frac{|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}}{T^{0}}-4)\sqrt{\mu_{0}},\quad\mathcal{B}_{2}^{0}=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2T^{0}}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})(\frac{|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}}{T^{0}}-4)\sqrt{\mu_{0}},\end{split}

and
\mathcal{A}_{21}^{0}:=\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},\mathcal{A}_{22}^{0}:=-\mathcal{A}_{11}^{0}.
A direct calculation shows

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{A}_{ij}^{0},\mathcal{A}_{ij}^{0}\rangle=\rho^{0},\quad\langle\mathcal{B}_{i}^{0},\mathcal{B}_{i}^{0}\rangle=\rho^{0},

and
\mathcal{A}_{11}^{0},\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0},\mathcal{B}_{2}^{0}
are orthogonal to each other and belong to
\mathcal{N}_{0}^{\perp}
. We also define

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{A}_{ij}^{0},\mathbf{L}_{0}^{-1}\mathcal{A}_{ij}^{0}\rangle=\frac{\kappa_{1}(T^{0})}{T^{0}},\quad\langle\mathcal{B}_{i}^{0},\mathbf{L}_{0}^{-1}\mathcal{B}_{i}^{0}\rangle=\frac{\kappa_{2}(T^{0})}{T^{0}}.

Noting (
1.24
), one has that

(6.3)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\langle 1,\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle=\bar{\rho}_{k},\quad\langle\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau},\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle=\bar{\rho}_{k}u_{\tau}^{0}+\rho^{0}\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau},\quad\langle\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n},\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle=\rho^{0}\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n},\\
&\langle|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2},\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle=\rho^{0}\bar{\theta}_{k}+2\bar{\rho}_{k}T^{0}=2\bar{p}_{k},\\

&\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}),\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle=\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})+T^{0}\langle\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{0})\left\{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}}{\sqrt{\mu_{0}}}\right\}\rangle.\end{split}

The equation (

) is formally written as

\displaystyle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})\partial_{y}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}=Q(\cdot,\cdot)-\partial_{t}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\right\}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}.

Integrating (

\bar{v}
, one has that

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}

(6.4)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}.

(6.5)

\displaystyle\quad+2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}.

Considering
\eqref{(1)}\times(-u_{\tau}^{0})+\eqref{(2)}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}-(\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\partial_{t}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})+(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\cdot\left\{\partial_{t}\rho^{0}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0}-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\bar{\rho}_{k}\cdot\left\{\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\right\}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\bar{p}_{k}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})

It follows from (

It is clear that

Noting

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2T^{0}}}\left\{\frac{\bar{\theta}_{k}}{T^{0}}(b\cdot\vec{n})\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0}+\frac{\bar{\theta}_{k}}{T^{0}}(b\cdot\vec{\tau})\mathcal{B}_{2}^{0}+\frac{c}{T^{0}}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0}+\frac{c}{T^{0}}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\mathcal{B}_{2}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{c\cdot\bar{\theta}_{k}}{16(T^{0})^{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{0})\left\{\left(\frac{|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}}{T^{0}}-4\right)^{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\right\}.

Using (

), one has

(6.12)

\displaystyle\quad+(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau}+\bar{u}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau})]+T^{0}\partial_{y}\langle\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},\bar{J}_{k-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\kappa_{1}(T^{0})\partial_{yy}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\rho^{0}\{\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\}\cdot(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{n})-\rho^{0}(\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})

\displaystyle\quad+\rho^{0}\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau}+\bar{u}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau})-\rho^{0}(\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau})(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})+T^{0}\partial_{y}\langle\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},\bar{J}_{k-1}\rangle,

where we have used

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\mu=\left\{\frac{\partial_{r}\rho}{\rho}+\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}\cdot\frac{\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}}{T}+\frac{\partial_{r}T}{2T}\left(\frac{|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}|^{2}}{T}-2\right)\right\}\mu,

and
\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}=\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}|_{r=1}
. Combining (
6.6
) and (
6.12
), one has that

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\partial_{t}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})-\kappa_{1}(T^{0})\partial_{yy}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})

\displaystyle-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\rho^{0}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{n})\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})

\displaystyle+(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\cdot\left\{\partial_{t}\rho^{0}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0}-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}-\rho^{0}\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}\right\}

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}_{k}\cdot\left\{\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\right\}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\bar{p}_{k}-\left(\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}+\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau}\right)\rho^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})

(6.13)

\displaystyle+\rho^{0}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau}+\bar{u}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau})\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})=\bar{W}_{k-1}-T^{0}\partial_{y}\langle\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},\bar{J}_{k-1}\rangle.

We rewrite the Euler equations (
1.9
) in polar coordinate

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\rho+\partial_{r}(\rho\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{n})+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\phi}(\rho\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\frac{1}{r}\rho(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{n})=0,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{\tau}+(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{n})\partial_{r}\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{\tau}+\frac{1}{r}(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{\tau})\partial_{\phi}(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\frac{1}{\rho r}\partial_{\phi}(\rho T)=0,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}T+\partial_{r}(T\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{n})+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\phi}(T\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\frac{1}{r}T(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\vec{n})=0,

which yields that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\rho^{0}=\rho^{0}\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}+\rho^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}+u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0}=u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}+\partial_{\phi}T^{0}+\frac{T^{0}}{\rho^{0}}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0},

(6.14)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}T^{0}=T^{0}\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}+T^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}+u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}T^{0}.

Combining (
6.13
)–(
6.14
), noting the definition of
\bar{p}_{k}
, we get

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\partial_{t}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})-\kappa_{1}(T^{0})\partial_{yy}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})

\displaystyle-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})+\rho^{0}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{n})\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})

\displaystyle+(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\cdot\left\{\left(1-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0}+\left(1-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)\rho^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{\rho^{0}}{2T^{0}}\bar{\theta}_{k}\cdot\left\{\left(1-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}+\partial_{\phi}T^{0}+\frac{T^{0}}{\rho^{0}}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\bar{p}_{k}}{T^{0}}\cdot\left\{\left(1-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}+\partial_{\phi}T^{0}+\frac{T^{0}}{\rho^{0}}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0}\right\}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\bar{p}_{k}

\displaystyle-\left(\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}+\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau}\right)\rho^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})+\rho^{0}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau}+\bar{u}_{1}\cdot\vec{\tau})\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})

\displaystyle=\bar{W}_{k-1}-T^{0}\partial_{y}\langle\mathcal{A}_{12}^{0},\bar{J}_{k-1}\rangle,

which concludes
\eqref{bu-0}_{1}
.

Step 2.

Multiplying (
1.22
) by
|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}
, one has

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}\,d\bar{v}\right\}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\{\partial_{t}(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2})\}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left\{\partial_{\phi}\left(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\right)\right\}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}.

Noting

\displaystyle\partial_{\phi}\left(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\right)=2u_{\tau}^{0}\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n}-2\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0}),\quad\partial_{t}\left(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\right)=-2\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0}),

we further derive

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}\,d\bar{v}\right\}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}-2(\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\quad-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}+2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

(6.15)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}.

Considering
(-4T^{0})\times\eqref{(1)}+\eqref{(3)}
, one obtains

\displaystyle\partial_{y}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}\,d\bar{v}\right\}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{t}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}-2(\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}-4(\partial_{t}T^{0})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\quad-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}+2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}+4\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}T^{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{u_{\tau}^{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v}\right\}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\,d\bar{v},

which yields that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\{-2\bar{p}_{k}+2\rho^{0}\bar{\theta}_{k}\right\}+4(\partial_{t}T^{0})\bar{\rho}_{k}+2(\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0})\rho^{0}\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau}

\displaystyle+2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)(\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0})\left\{\bar{p}_{k}+\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\right\}

\displaystyle-4\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)(\partial_{\phi}T^{0})(\bar{\rho}_{k}u_{\tau}^{0}+\rho^{0}\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\left\{-2u_{\tau}^{0}\bar{p}_{k}+2\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}\bar{\theta}_{k}\right\}

(6.16)

\displaystyle+\partial_{y}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0})\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}\,d\bar{v}\right\}=\bar{H}_{k-1},

where

\displaystyle\bar{H}_{k-1}=

\displaystyle-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0}),\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0}),\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle

(6.17)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0}),\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{\tau}-u_{\tau}^{0})^{2}-(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})^{2},\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k}\rangle.

To calculate
\partial_{y}\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0}),\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}\rangle
, we have from (
6.8
) that

\displaystyle\langle(\bar{v}\cdot\vec{n})(|\bar{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-4T^{0}),\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}\rangle

\displaystyle=(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\left\langle\mathcal{B}^{0}_{1},\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{0}\}\left\{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{F}}_{k+1}}{\sqrt{\mu_{0}}}\right\}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle\mathcal{B}^{0}_{1},\bar{P}_{1}\rangle+(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle\mathcal{B}^{0}_{1},\bar{P}_{2}+\bar{P}_{3}+\bar{P}_{4}\rangle+(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle\bar{J}_{k-1},\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=-2\kappa_{2}(T^{0})\partial_{y}\bar{\theta}_{k}+2\rho^{0}\bar{\theta}_{k}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}-u^{0}_{1}\cdot\vec{n})

(6.18)

\displaystyle\quad+2\rho^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})\cdot(-2y\partial_{r}T^{0}+\theta_{1}^{0}+\bar{\theta}_{1})+(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\langle\bar{J}_{k-1},\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0}\rangle.

Combining (
6.16
) and (
6.18
), one has that

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\partial_{t}\bar{\theta}_{k}-\kappa_{2}(T^{0})\partial_{yy}\bar{\theta}_{k}+\rho^{0}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}-u_{1}^{0}\cdot\vec{n})\partial_{y}\bar{\theta}_{k}

\displaystyle-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\bar{\theta}_{k}+\bar{\theta}_{k}\left\{\partial_{t}\rho^{0}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}(\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0})-\rho^{0}\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}\right\}

\displaystyle+\rho^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\left\{\partial_{t}u_{\tau}^{0}-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\partial_{\phi}T^{0}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\bar{p}_{k}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\bar{p}_{k}+\bar{\rho}_{k}\left\{2\partial_{t}T^{0}-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}T^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle+\rho^{0}\left\{\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2}-2\partial_{r}T^{0}\right\}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})+\rho^{0}(-2y\partial_{r}T^{0}+\theta_{1}^{0}+\bar{\theta}_{1})\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bar{H}_{k-1}-\frac{1}{2}(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{y}\langle\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0},\bar{J}_{k-1}\rangle,

which together with (
6.14
) and the definition of
\bar{p}_{k}
yields that

\displaystyle\rho^{0}\partial_{t}\bar{\theta}_{k}-\kappa_{2}(T^{0})\partial_{yy}\bar{\theta}_{k}+\rho^{0}(-y\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}-u_{1}^{0}\cdot\vec{n})\partial_{y}\bar{\theta}_{k}-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\bar{\theta}_{k}

\displaystyle+\bar{\theta}_{k}\left\{\left(1-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)\partial_{\phi}(\rho^{0}u_{\tau}^{0})-\frac{\rho^{0}}{T^{0}}\left(1-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}T^{0}-\rho^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}-\rho^{0}\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}\right\}

\displaystyle+\rho^{0}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{\tau})\left\{\left(1-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)\partial_{\phi}T^{0}+\frac{T^{0}}{\rho^{0}}\partial_{\phi}\rho^{0}+\left(1-\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}\right\}

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\bar{p}_{k}+\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}\bar{p}_{k}+\frac{\bar{p}_{k}}{T^{0}}\left\{\left(2-2\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)\right)u_{\tau}^{0}\partial_{\phi}T^{0}+2T^{0}\partial_{\phi}u_{\tau}^{0}+2T^{0}\partial_{r}\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot\vec{n}\right\}

\displaystyle+\rho^{0}\left\{\mathcal{H}^{\varepsilon}(y)|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2}-2\partial_{r}T^{0}\right\}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})+\rho^{0}(-2y\partial_{r}T^{0}+\theta_{1}^{0}+\bar{\theta}_{1})\partial_{y}(\bar{u}_{k}\cdot\vec{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bar{H}_{k-1}-\frac{1}{2}(2T^{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{y}\langle\mathcal{B}_{1}^{0},\bar{J}_{k-1}\rangle,

which is exactly
\eqref{bu-0}_{2}
.
\hfill\Box

6.2.
Proof of Lemma
2.9

Recall
\hat{S}_{k,1}
in (
2.14
), the condition
\eqref{eq2.24}_{1}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}\right\}+G(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}=\rho^{0}\hat{a}_{k},

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}^{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}\right\}+G(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\mathfrak{v}_{1}^{2}-\mathfrak{v}_{2}^{2})\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}=\rho^{0}\hat{b}_{k,1},

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}\mathfrak{v}_{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}\right\}+2G(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}\mathfrak{v}_{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}=\rho^{0}(\hat{b}_{k,2}+u_{\tau}^{0}\hat{a}_{k}),

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}\right\}+G(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathfrak{v}_{1}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu_{0}}\hat{f}_{k,1}\,d\mathfrak{v}

\displaystyle=\rho^{0}\left\{(2T^{0}+|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2})\hat{a}_{k}+2u_{\tau}^{0}\hat{b}_{k,2}+4T^{0}\hat{c}_{k}\right\}.

Noting the construction of
\hat{f}_{k,1}
in (
2.15
), by serious calculation, one has that

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\hat{A}_{k}\cdot e^{-W(\eta)}\right\}=\hat{a}_{k}\cdot e^{-W(\eta)},

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\hat{D}_{k}\cdot e^{\frac{|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2}}{T^{0}}W(\eta)}\right\}=\frac{\hat{b}_{k,1}}{T^{0}}\cdot e^{\frac{|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2}}{T^{0}}W(\eta)},

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\left(\hat{B}_{k}+\frac{u_{\tau}^{0}}{T^{0}}\hat{A}_{k}\right)\cdot e^{-2W(\eta)}\right\}=\left(\frac{\hat{b}_{k,2}+u_{\tau}^{0}\hat{a}_{k}}{T^{0}}\right)\cdot e^{-2W(\eta)},

\displaystyle\partial_{\eta}\left\{\left[8(T^{0})^{2}\hat{C}_{k}+2u_{\tau}^{0}T^{0}\hat{B}_{k}+(4T^{0}+|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2})\hat{A}_{k}\right]\cdot e^{-W(\eta)}\right\}

(6.19)

\displaystyle=\left\{(2T^{0}+|u_{\tau}^{0}|^{2})\hat{a}_{k}+2u_{\tau}^{0}\hat{b}_{k,2}+4T^{0}\hat{c}_{k}\right\}\cdot e^{-W(\eta)}.

Assuming
\hat{A}_{k}(d)=\hat{B}_{k}(d)=\hat{C}_{k}(d)=\hat{D}_{k}(d)\equiv 0
, integrating (
6.19
) on
[\eta,d]
, one can prove Lemma
2.9
.
\hfill\Box

6.3.
Proof of Lemma
4.1

We first prove that

k_{M}(\mathfrak{v},u)e^{\zeta(|u|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})}\cdot\frac{(1+|u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}{(1+|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\in L_{u}^{1}.

One has that

\displaystyle k_{M,i}(\mathfrak{v},u)=k_{i}(\mathfrak{v},u)\cdot e^{\frac{1}{4T^{0}}(|u-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2})}\cdot e^{\frac{1}{4T_{M}}(|\mathfrak{v}|^{2}-|u|^{2})},

which yields that

\displaystyle k_{M,i}(\mathfrak{v},u)\cdot e^{\zeta(|u|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})}=k_{i}(\mathfrak{v},u)\cdot e^{(\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}-\zeta)(|\mathfrak{v}|^{2}-|u|^{2})}\cdot e^{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}}{2T^{0}}\cdot(\mathfrak{v}-u)}.

Noting
\frac{(1+|u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}{(1+|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\leq(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
, recall (
4.5
), we get that

\displaystyle k_{M,1}(\mathfrak{v},u)\cdot e^{\zeta(|u|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})}\cdot\frac{(1+|u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}{(1+|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,k_{1}(\mathfrak{v},u)(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot e^{(\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}-\zeta)(|\mathfrak{v}|^{2}-|u|^{2})}\cdot e^{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}}{2T^{0}}\cdot(\mathfrak{v}-u)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|)^{\beta+1}e^{-(\frac{1}{2T^{0}}-\frac{1}{4T_{M}}+\zeta)|\mathfrak{v}|^{2}}e^{-(\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\zeta)|u|^{2}}\cdot e^{\frac{\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}}{T^{0}}\mathfrak{v}}\quad\in L^{1}_{u}\cap L^{2}_{u},

which yields that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}k_{M,1}(\mathfrak{v},u)e^{\zeta(|u|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})}\cdot\frac{(1+|u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}{(1+|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\,du\leq C(1+|\mathfrak{v})^{-1}.

A direct calculation gives

\displaystyle k_{M,2}(\mathfrak{v},u)e^{\zeta(|u|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})}\cdot(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}

(6.20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k_{2}(\mathfrak{v},u)(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot e^{(\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}-\zeta)(|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-|u-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2})}\cdot e^{(\frac{1}{2T_{M}}-2\zeta)\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot(\mathfrak{v}-u)}.

Noting (
1.34
), we have

-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}<\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}-\zeta<\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}<\frac{1}{4T^{0}},

then it follows from (
4.5
) and
[
23
]
that

\displaystyle k_{M,2}(\mathfrak{v},u)e^{\zeta(|u|^{2}-|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})}\cdot\frac{(1+|u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}{(1+|\mathfrak{v}|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{8T^{0}}\left(|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2}+\frac{\left||\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-|u-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\right|^{2}}{|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\exp\left\{\left|\frac{1}{4T_{M}}-\frac{1}{4T^{0}}-\zeta\right|\cdot\left||\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-|u-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\right|\right\}\exp\left\{(\frac{1}{2T_{M}}-2\zeta)\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}\cdot(\mathfrak{v}-u)\right\}

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle L_{u}^{1}\cap L^{2}_{u}.

Then we only need to prove that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}k_{M,2}(\mathfrak{v},u)(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}du\leq C(1+|\mathfrak{v}|)^{-1}.

When
|\mathfrak{v}|\leq C_{0}
with
C_{0}
a constant satisfying
C_{0}\gg|u_{\tau}^{0}|
, the above equation obviously holds. If
|\mathfrak{v}|\geq C_{0},
we have from (
4.5
) and (
6.20
) that there exists a
c_{1}>0
such that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}k_{M,2}(\mathfrak{v},u)(1+|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}du

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{1}}{2}|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2}-c_{1}\frac{\left||\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}-|u-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\right|^{2}}{|\mathfrak{v}-u|^{2}}\right\}\,du

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\pi}r\exp\left\{-\frac{c_{1}}{2}r^{2}-c_{1}(r+2|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|\cos\theta)^{2}\right\}\,d\theta dr

\displaystyle=C\int_{0}^{\infty}r\exp\left\{-\frac{3c_{1}}{2}(r+\frac{4}{3}|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|\cos\theta)^{2}\right\}dr\cdot\int_{0}^{\pi}\exp\left\{-\frac{4}{3}c_{1}|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\cos^{2}\theta\right\}d\theta

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{4}}\exp\left\{-\frac{4}{3}c_{1}|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\cos^{2}\theta\right\}d\theta+C\int_{\frac{\pi}{4}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\exp\left\{-\frac{4}{3}c_{1}|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|^{2}\cos^{2}\theta\right\}\sin\theta\,d\theta

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{|\mathfrak{v}-\bar{\mathfrak{u}}^{0}|}.

Hence we can easily get (
4.7
). Therefore the proof of Lemma
4.1
is completed.
\hfill\Box
